## The ticket

Make a multi-turn agent conversation a durable, resumable record: the seed becomes a real logged turn, resume state is derived from the log instead of a step parity, and the resumed run neither re-generates nor re-counts what it already has.

### New module `src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/turn_ledger.py`

- Module docstring: `"""The durable record of a multi-turn agent conversation."""`. It takes every timestamp as a parameter — it imports neither `time`, `random`, `uuid` nor `os.urandom`.
- Constants: `RESPONSES_FILENAME: str = "responses_0.jsonl"`, `SEED_FINISH_REASON: str = "seed"`, `COMPLETION_SENTINEL: str`, `COMPLETION_REASONS: tuple[str, ...] = ("open", "budget", "agent_signal")`, `LEDGER_STATUSES: tuple[str, ...] = ("created", "adopted", "verified")`.
- Exceptions: `TurnLedgerError(RuntimeError)` as the base for every turn-ledger failure, and `TurnLedgerCorruptError(TurnLedgerError)` with `__init__(self, path: str, line_number: int, reason: str)` storing `.path`, `.line_number` (1-based over physical lines) and `.reason`.
- Two **frozen dataclasses** (`@dataclass(frozen=True)` — not pydantic models, not `NamedTuple`s, not dicts), field order exactly:
  - `TurnEntry(turn, author, content, source)` — `turn` is the 0-based index into the log (the seed is `0`), `author` is the agent name the line was written under, `content` is `response_message` coerced with `str()` when it is not already a `str`, `source` is `"seed"` for turn 0 and `"response"` otherwise.
  - `TurnLedger(entries, seeder_name, partner_name, max_responses, responses, turns, next_speaker, last_author, interleave_faults, completed, completion_reason, status)`, where `entries` is a `tuple[TurnEntry, ...]` and never a `list`.
- `TurnLedger.messages() -> list[dict[str, str]]` returns `[{"role": entry.author, "content": entry.content}, ...]` in log order.
- Functions:
  - `build_seed_record(*, seeder_name: str, seed_message: str, model_name: str, now: datetime.datetime) -> AgentResponse`
  - `read_log(working_dir: str) -> list[AgentResponse]`
  - `build_ledger(records: t.Sequence[AgentResponse], *, seeder_name: str, partner_name: str, max_responses: int, is_completed: t.Callable[[str, t.Any], bool], status: str = "verified") -> TurnLedger`
  - `load_ledger(working_dir: str, *, seeder_name: str, partner_name: str, max_responses: int, is_completed: t.Callable[[str, t.Any], bool]) -> TurnLedger` — loads the ledger for a working directory and never writes to disk.

### Ledger semantics

- `responses == len(entries) - 1`, never negative; `turns == len(entries)`.
- `next_speaker` is the agent that did **not** write the last entry: `partner_name if last_author == seeder_name else seeder_name`. It is never computed from `len(entries) % 2`, from `responses % 2`, or from a step counter. An entry whose author is neither `seeder_name` nor `partner_name` counts as "not the seeder", so `next_speaker` becomes `seeder_name`.
- A log in which the same author appears twice in a row is **legal**: it does not raise, does not truncate the log, and does not drop the duplicate. `interleave_faults` is the number of indices `i >= 1` with `entries[i].author == entries[i - 1].author`, and the next turn still goes to the complement of the last author.
- `completion_reason` is `"agent_signal"` when the **last** entry's author's `is_completed` returns true for its content (checked on the last entry only, not on every entry); otherwise `"budget"` when `responses >= max_responses`; otherwise `"open"`. `"agent_signal"` wins when both hold. `completed` is `completion_reason != "open"`, and `next_speaker is None` exactly when `completed` is true.
- An empty log gives `(completion_reason, responses, turns, last_author, next_speaker) == ("open", 0, 0, None, None)` with `completed is False`.
- `read_log` skips lines that are empty or whitespace-only and raises `TurnLedgerCorruptError(path, line_number, reason)` for any other unparseable line, instead of letting pydantic's `ValidationError` out.

### `MultiTurnAgenticProcessor` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/processor.py`)

- `__init__(self, seeder, partner, max_length, seed_message, now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = datetime.datetime.now)`; keeps `self.now_fn` and `self.ledger: t.Optional[TurnLedger] = None`. `AgentStatusTracker(max_turns=self.max_length)` is unchanged.
- `load_cache(self, working_dir: str) -> TurnLedger` returns a `TurnLedger` and does not touch the status tracker at all. The processor keeps the last ledger it built on `self.ledger`.
- **The seed is a durable turn.** When `responses_0.jsonl` is absent or empty, `run()` writes exactly one line — `build_seed_record(...)` — through the existing `append_response()` *before* the first request is built, then enters the loop. That record has `name = seeder.name`, `response_message = seed_message`, `finish_reason = "seed"`, `response_cost = 0.0`, `token_usage = None`, `response_errors = None`, `raw_response = None`, `raw_request = None`, `parsed_response_message = None`, `created_at = finished_at = now_fn()`, and `generic_request = GenericRequest(model=seeder.model_name, messages=[{"role": "user", "content": seed_message}], original_row={"prompt": seed_message}, original_row_idx=0, response_format=None, generation_params={}, is_multimodal_prompt=False)`.
- **`max_length` is a budget of generated responses, not of messages.** The seed is not one. The loop condition is `while ledger.responses < self.max_length`, so an un-stopped run makes exactly `max_length` calls to `call_single_request` and leaves `max_length + 1` log lines whether it ran once or resumed five times. `APIRequest(task_id=...)` is given `ledger.responses`, the 0-based response index.
- Each iteration asks the agent named by `ledger.next_speaker`; `_agent_for(name)` maps a name to `seeder`/`partner` and raises `KeyError` otherwise. `run()` short-circuits on `ledger.completed`, never on a message count reaching `max_length`.
- `self.conversation_history` stays a `list[dict[str, str]]` keyed `"role"` (an agent *name*) and `"content"`, and equals `self.ledger.messages()` after every ledger update.
- `run()` calls `status_tracker.adopt_ledger(cached_responses=ledger.responses)` exactly once, immediately after the ledger is loaded and **before** the `ledger.completed` short-circuit.
- A failed turn appends nothing to the log, is recorded against the agent named by `ledger.next_speaker` (not by step parity), and the exception is still re-raised.
- `_transform_conversation_history(target_agent)` maps every ledger message to `{"role": "assistant"}` when its author equals `target.name` and `{"role": "user"}` otherwise — uniformly, with **no special case for a one-message history**. It calls `target.prompt_formatter.create_generic_request({"prompt": <content of the last message>}, 0)`, splits that result into the system message and the rest, replaces the last mapped message with the rest, and inserts the system message at index 0 **only if there is one**; an agent built without `system_prompt=` must not raise. When the formatter produced more than one system message, the first is used and the others stay in place in the body.
- `create_dataset_file(working_dir)` writes one row per log line with exactly four fields: `{"role": <the record's name>, "content": <str(response_message)>, "turn": <0-based line index>, "source": "seed" if index == 0 else "response"}`. `source` is decided by position, not by `finish_reason`. Row order is log order.

### `AgentStatusTracker` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/status_tracker/agent_status_tracker.py`)

- New fields `num_cached: int = 0` and `time_fn: t.Callable[[], float] = field(default=time.time, repr=False, compare=False)`; `start_time` and `last_update_time` come from `time_fn`.
- `update_turn(agent, response_success=False, ...)` increments **only** `num_errors` — `current_turn` and `num_responses` are left alone — while still setting `current_agent` and still accumulating any token/cost arguments given.
- New `adopt_ledger(self, *, cached_responses: int) -> None`: sets `current_turn = cached_responses`, `num_cached = cached_responses`, `num_responses = 0`, `num_errors = 0`, `total_cost = 0.0` and all three `total_tokens` counters to `0`; it does not touch `max_turns` and does not call `update_display`. So `num_responses`/`total_cost` describe *this process*, `num_cached` describes what was inherited, and `current_turn` describes the conversation.
- `stop_tracker()` removes `"time_fn"` from the telemetry metadata dict alongside `"pbar"`.

### `Agent` / `MultiTurnAgents` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/agent.py`)

- `Agent.is_completed(self, response: t.Any) -> bool` currently returns `False` unconditionally, which makes the processor's stop-condition path dead code in every stock use. Give it a real default implementation in terms of `COMPLETION_SENTINEL`.
- `MultiTurnAgents.__init__(self, seeder, partner, max_length, seed_message, *, now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = datetime.datetime.now)` accepts and forwards `now_fn`.

### `MultiTurnResponse` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/agent_response.py`)

- `update_tracker_stats()` reports `RequestStats(total=tracker.max_turns, succeeded=tracker.num_responses, failed=tracker.num_errors, in_progress=0, cached=tracker.num_cached)`.

### Reuse, scope and dependencies

- Reuse `AgentResponse` and its `model_validate_json`/`model_dump` (the seed is written as an `AgentResponse`, no new record type), `GenericRequest`, `MultiTurnAgenticProcessor.append_response()` verbatim, `PromptFormatter.create_generic_request()` (read, never modified), `AgentTurn`, `_TokenUsage`, `ArrowWriter`/`Dataset.from_file` as already used, and `APIRequest` constructed exactly as today.
- Nothing in `llm/`, `request_processor/`, `client.py`, `db.py` or the viewer changes. `Agent._hash_fingerprint` and the `xxh64(seed_message)` run identity are untouched.
- Python `^3.10`; **no new dependency** — `pydantic`, `datasets`, `aiofiles`, `aiohttp` and stdlib `json`/`os`/`datetime`/`typing` are all already present. Tests use `pytest`, `pytest-asyncio`, `tmp_path`, `monkeypatch` and `types.SimpleNamespace`; no network, no real provider.

</problem>

spent{END} • \\"\\n                    f\\"{METRIC}{self.total_tokens.total:,} tokens{END}]\\"\\n                )\\n                self.pbar.refresh()\\n\\n        self.last_update_time = current_time\\n\\n    def _refresh_console(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Refresh the console display with latest stats.\\"\\"\\"\\n        # Calculate stats\\n        elapsed_minutes = (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60\\n        input_tpm = self.total_tokens.input / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        output_tpm = self.total_tokens.output / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        cost_per_minute = self.total_cost / max(0.01, elapsed_minutes)\\n\\n        # Format stats text\\n        stats_text = (\\n            f\\"[bold white]Conversation:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Turn:[/white] [blue]{self.current_turn}/{self.max_turns}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Agent:[/white] [blue]{self.current_agent.value}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Responses:[/white] [green]{self.num_responses}[/green] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n self.start_time:.1f}s[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Tokens:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input:[/white] [blue]{self.total_tokens.input:,}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"([blue]{input_tpm:.0f}/min[/blue]) \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output:[/white] [blue]{self.total_tokens.output:,}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"([blue]{output_tpm:.0f}/min[/blue]) \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Total:[/white] [blue]{self.total_tokens.total:,}[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Cost:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Total:[/white] [red]${self.total_cost:.3f}[/red] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Rate:[/white] [red]${cost_per_minute:.3f}/min[/red]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Model Pricing:[/bold white]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"  [white]Seeder ({self.seeder_model}):[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input:[/white] {self.input_cost_str[\'seeder\']} \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output:[/white] {self.output_cost_str[\'seeder\']}\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"  [white]Partner ({self.partner_model}):[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input:[/white] {self.input_cost_str[\'partner\']} \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output:[/white] {self.output_cost_str[\'partner\']}\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update main progress bar\\n        self._progress.update(\\n            self._task_id,\\n            completed=self.current_turn,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update stats display\\n        self._stats.update(\\n            self._stats_task_id,\\n            description=stats_text,\\n        )\\n\\n    def stop_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Stop the tracker and display final statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            if hasattr(self, \\"_live\\"):\\n                self._live.stop()\\n                self._console.print(self._progress)\\n                self._console.print(self._stats)\\n        else:\\n            if self.pbar:\\n                self.pbar.close()\\n\\n        # Display final statistics\\n        self.display_final_stats()\\n\\n        # Clean up non-serializable fields before telemetry\\n        temp_pbar = self.pbar\\n What should I do?\\"\\n\\n    # Create the multi-turn conversation simulator\\n    simulator = MultiTurnAgents(\\n        seeder=client,\\n        partner=advisor,\\n        max_length=4,  # 2 turns each\\n        seed_message=seed_message,\\n    )\\n\\n    with vcr_config.use_cassette(\\"basic_agent_conversation.yaml\\"):\\n        result = simulator(working_dir=temp_working_dir)\\n\\n        assert len(result.dataset) == 4\\n        df = result.dataset.to_pandas()\\n        convo = \\"\\".join([recipe[0] for recipe in df.values.tolist()])\\n        hash_convo = hashlib.sha256(convo.encode(\\"utf-8\\")).hexdigest()\\n        assert hash_convo == \\"072b0624b1c4f74ad2d2da9952ca14580edbc150a569f59e4d5c5b451568dd27\\"\\n        assert \\"content\\" in df.columns\\n        assert \\"role\\" in df.columns",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

Attributes:\\n        model_name: Name of the LLM model to use\\n        prompt_func: Function that takes input and returns formatted prompts\\n        parse_func: Optional function to parse model responses\\n        response_format: Optional Pydantic model defining expected response format\\n        generation_params: Dictionary of parameters for generation\\n        system_prompt: Optional system prompt to use for the LLM\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    model_name: str\\n    prompt_func: Callable[[_DictOrBaseModel], Dict[str, str]]\\n    parse_func: Optional[Callable[[_DictOrBaseModel, _DictOrBaseModel], T]] = None\\n    response_format: Optional[Type[BaseModel]] = None\\n    generation_params: dict = field(default_factory=dict)\\n    system_prompt: str | None = None\\n\\n    def get_prompts(self, row: _DictOrBaseModel) -> str | list[str]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get the prompts for the given row.\\"\\"\\"\\n        sig = inspect.signature(self.prompt_func)\\n        if len(sig.parameters) == 0:\\n            return self.prompt_func()\\n        if len(sig.parameters) == 1:\\n            if isinstance(row, dict):\\n                if _INTERNAL_PROMPT_KEY in row:\\n                    return self.prompt_func(row[_INTERNAL_PROMPT_KEY])\\n            return self.prompt_func(row)\\n        raise ValueError(f\\"Prompting function {self.prompt_func} must have 0 or 1 arguments.\\")\\n\\n    def get_messages(self, prompts: str | list[str]) -> list[dict]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get the messages for the given prompts.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if isinstance(prompts, str):\\n            return [{\\"role\\": \\"user\\", \\"content\\": prompts}]\\n        elif isinstance(prompts, list):\\n            _validate_messages(prompts)\\n            return prompts\\n        elif isinstance(prompts, tuple):\\n            return [{\\"role\\": \\"user\\", \\"content\\": _MultiModalPrompt.load(prompts)}]\\n        raise ValueError(f\\"The return value of the `prompt` method {type(prompts)} did not match the expected format.\\")\\n\\n    def _put_system_prompt(self, messages: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:\\n        if self.system_prompt:\\n            if any(msg[\\"role\\"] == \\"system\\" for msg in messages):\\n                raise ValueError(\\"System prompt already exists in the messages\\")\\n            messages.insert(0, {\\"role\\": \\"system\\", \\"content\\": self.system_prompt})\\n        return messages\\n\\n    def create_generic_request(self, row: _DictOrBaseModel, idx: int, generation_params_per_row: bool = False) -> GenericRequest:",\n  "error": usage.\n    """\n    generic_request = GenericRequest(\n        model=model_name,\n        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": seed_message}],\n        original_row={"prompt": seed_message},\n seeder_name,\n            "response_message": seed_message,\n            "finish_reason": SEED_FINISH_REASON,\n            "response_cost": 0.0,\n            "token_usage": None,\n            "response_errors": None,\n            "raw_response": None,\n            "raw_request": None,\n            "parsed_response_message": None,\n            "created_at": now,\n            "finished_at": now,\n            "generic_request": generic_request,\n        }\n    )\n\n\ndef read_log(working_dir: str) -> list[AgentResponse]:\n    """Read the JSONL turn log for a working directory.\n\n    Empty or whitespace-only lines are skipped. Any other unparseable line raises\n    ``TurnLedgerCorruptError`` instead of leaking pydantic\'s ``ValidationError``.\n    """\n    path = os.path.join(working_dir, RESPONSES_FILENAME)\n    records: list[AgentResponse] = []\n    if not os.path.exists(path):\n        return records\n    with open(path, "r") as f:\n        line_number = 0\n        for line in f:\n            line_number += 1\n            if not line.strip():\n                continue\n            try:\n                records.append(AgentResponse.model_validate_json(line))\n            except ValidationError as exc:\n                raise TurnLedgerCorruptError(\n                    path=path,\n                    line_number=line_number,\n                    reason=str(exc),\n                ) from exc\n            except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive\n                raise TurnLedgerCorruptError(\n                    path=path,\n                    line_number=line_number,\n                    reason=str(exc),\n                ) from exc\n    return records\n\n\ndef build_ledger(\n    records: t.Sequence[AgentResponse],\n    *,\n    seeder_name: str,\n    partner_name: str,\n    max_responses: int,\n    is_completed: t.Callable[[str, t.Any], bool],\n    status: str = "verified",\n) -> TurnLedger:\n    """Build a :class:`TurnLedger` from a sequence of logged ``AgentResponse`` records.\n\n    All resume state is derived from the log rather than from a step parity:\n    ``next_speaker`` is the complement of the last author, ``responses`` is\n    ``len(entries) - 1``, and ``completion_reason`` is checked on the last entry only.\n    """\n    entries: list[TurnEntry] = []\n    for index, record in enumerate(records):\n        name = record.name\n        content = record.response_message\n        if not isinstance(content, str):\n            content = str(content)\n        source = "seed" if index == 0 else "response"\n        entries.append(\n            TurnEntry(turn=index, author=name, content=content, source=source)\n        )\n\n    turns = len(entries)\n    responses = turns - 1].author:\n            interleave_faults += 1\n\n    completion_reason = "open"\n    if entries:\n        last_content: t.Any = entries[-1].content\n        if is_completed(last_author, last_content):\n            completion_reason = "agent_signal"\n        elif responses >= max_responses:\n            completion_reason = "budget"\n    else:\n        if responses >= max_responses:\n            completion_reason = "budget"\n\n    completed = completion_reason != "open"\n\n    next_speaker: t.Optional[str] = None\n    if not completed:\n        if last_author is None:\n            next_speaker = None\n        elif last_author == seeder_name:\n            next_speaker = partner_name\n        else:\n            # An entry whose author is neither seeder nor partner counts as\n            # "not the seeder", so the next turn goes to the seeder.\n            next_speaker = seeder_name\n\n    return TurnLedger(\n        entries=tuple(entries),\n        seeder_name=seeder_name,\n        partner_name=partner_name,\n        max_responses=max_responses,\n        responses=responses,\n        turns=turns,\n        next_speaker=next_speaker,\n        last_author=last_author,\n        interleave_faults=interleave_faults,\n        completed=completed,\n        completion_reason=completion_reason,\n        status=status,\n    )\n\n\ndef load_ledger(\n    working_dir: str,\n    *,\n    seeder_name: str,\n    partner_name: str,\n    max_responses: int,\n    is_completed: t.Callable[[str, t.Any], bool],\n) -> TurnLedger:\n    """Load the ledger for a working directory, deriving state from the log.\n\n    This reads the log and builds a ledger; it never writes to disk.\n    """\n    records = read_log(working_dir)\n    return build_ledger(\n        records,\n        seeder_name=seeder_name,\n        partner_name=partner_name,\n        max_responses=max_responses,\n        is_completed=is_completed,\n    )\n'}

The\n        ``current_agent`` is still recorded and any token/cost arguments are\n        still accumulated.\n\n        Args:\n            agent (AgentTurn): The agent that just took a turn.\n            response_success (bool): Whether the response was successful.\n            token_usage (Optional[_TokenUsage]): Token usage for this turn.\n            cost (Optional[float]): Cost for this turn.\n        """\n        self.current_agent = agent\n        if not response_success:\n            self.num_errors += 1\n\n        if token_usage:\n            self.total_tokens.input += token_usage.input\n            self.total_tokens.output += token_usage.output\n            self.total_tokens.total += token_usage.total\n\n        if cost:\n            self.total_cost += cost\n\n        self.update_display()\n\n    def adopt_ledger(self, *, cached_responses: int) -> None:\n        """Adopt the resume state inherited from the turn ledger.\n\n        Resets the per-process counters (``num_responses``, ``num_errors``,\n        ``total_cost`` and the token counters) so they describe *this* process,\n        records the inherited responses in ``num_cached`` and ``current_turn``,\n        and leaves ``max_turns`` untouched. It does not call\n        :meth:`update_display`.\n\n        Args:\n            cached_responses: The number of responses already present in the log.\n        """\n        self.current_turn = cached_responses\n        self.num_cached = cached_responses\n        self.num_responses = 0\n        self.num_errors = 0\n        self.total_cost = 0.0\n        self.total_tokens.input = 0\n        self.total_tokens.output = 0\n        self.total_tokens.total = 0', 'old_str': '    def update_turn(self, agent: AgentTurn, response_success: bool = True, token_usage: t.Optional[_TokenUsage] = None, cost: t.Optional[float] = None):\n        """Update the tracker with a new turn.\n\n        Args:\n            agent (AgentTurn): The agent that just took a turn.\n            response_success (bool): Whether the response was successful.\n            token_usage (Optional[_TokenUsage]): Token usage for this turn.\n            cost (Optional[float]): Cost for this turn.\n        """\n        self.current_turn += 1\n        self.current_agent = agent\n        self.num_responses += 1\n        if not response_success:\n            self.num_errors += 1\n\n        if token_usage:\n            self.total_tokens.input += token_usage.input\n            self.total_tokens.output += token_usage.output\n            self.total_tokens.total += token_usage.total\n\n        if cost:\n            self.total_cost += cost\n\n        self.update_display()', 'path': '/workdir/curator/src/bespokelabs/curator/status_tracker/agent_status_tracker.py'}

RESPONSES_FILENAME,\n    SEED_FINISH_REASON,\n    TurnLedger,\n    build_seed_record,\n    load_ledger,\n    read_log,\n)\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.log import logger\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.types.generic_response import GenericResponse\n\nif t.TYPE_CHECKING:\n    from bespokelabs.curator.agent.agent import Agent\n\n\nclass MultiTurnAgenticProcessor:\n    """A processor that manages multi-turn conversations between two agents.\n\n    The conversation is a durable, resumable record: the seed becomes a real\n    logged turn, resume state is derived from the log (via :class:`TurnLedger`)\n    instead of a step parity, and a resumed run neither re-generates nor\n    re-counts what it already has.\n\n    Attributes:\n        seeder (Agent): The agent that initiates the conversation.\n        partner (Agent): The agent that responds to the seeder.\n        max_length (int): The maximum number of turns in the conversation.\n        seed_message (str): The initial message to start the conversation.\n        conversation_history (list): List of message dictionaries containing role and content.\n        status_tracker (AgentStatusTracker): Tracks the status of the conversation.\n        now_fn: Callable returning the current datetime, used to timestamp the seed turn.\n        ledger: The last :class:`TurnLedger` built for this run, or ``None``.\n    """\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        seeder: "Agent",\n        partner: "Agent",\n        max_length: int,\n        seed_message: str,\n        now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = datetime.datetime.now,\n    ):\n        """Initialize a MultiTurnAgenticProcessor instance.\n\n        Args:\n            seeder (Agent): The agent that initiates the conversation.\n            partner (Agent): The agent that responds to the seeder.\n            max_length (int): The maximum number of turns in the conversation.\n            seed_message (str): The initial message to start the conversation.\n            now_fn: Callable returning the current datetime, used to timestamp\n                the durable seed turn.\n        """\n        self.seeder = seeder\n        self.partner = partner\n        self.max_length = max_length\n        self.seed_message = seed_message\n        self.conversation_history: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n        self.now_fn = now_fn\n        self.ledger: t.Optional[TurnLedger] = None\n\n        # Create status tracker with model information\n        self.status_tracker = AgentStatusTracker(\n            seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\n            partner_name=self.partner.name,\n            max_turns=self.max_length,\n            seeder_model=self.seeder.model_name,  # Use seeder\'s model for cost tracking\n            partner_model=self.partner.model_name,  # Use partner\'s model for cost tracking\n        )\n\n    def _is_completed(self, author: str, content: t.Any) -> bool:\n        """Return whether the named agent signals completion for ``content``."""\n        agent = self._agent_for(author)\n        return agent.is_completed(content)\n\n    def _agent_for(self, name: str) -> "Agent":\n        """Map an agent name to the seeder or partner agent.\n\n        Raises:\n            KeyError: If ``name`` is neither the seeder\'s nor the partner\'s name.\n        """\n        if name == self.seeder.name:\n            return self.seeder\n        if name == self.partner.name:\n            return self.partner\n        raise KeyError(name)\n\n    def _agent_turn_for(self, name: str) -> AgentTurn:\n        """Map an agent name to the matching :class:`AgentTurn` enum value."""\n        if name == self.seeder.name:\n            return AgentTurn.SEEDER\n        return AgentTurn.PARTNER\n\n    def load_cache(self, working_dir: str) -> TurnLedger:\n        """Load the durable turn ledger for a working directory.\n\n        The ledger is derived from the log on disk; resume state comes from the\n        ledger rather than a step parity. This does not touch the status tracker.\n\n        Args:\n            working_dir (str): Directory containing the cached conversation file.\n\n        Returns:\n            TurnLedger: The ledger describing the logged conversation so far.\n        """\n        ledger = load_ledger(\n            working_dir,\n            seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\n            partner_name=self.partner.name,\n            max_responses=self.max_length,\n            is_completed=self._is_completed,\n        )\n        self.ledger = ledger\n        self.conversation_history = ledger.messages()\n        return ledger\n\n    async def run(self, working_dir: str) -> Dataset:\n        """Execute the multi-turn conversation between agents.\n\n        The seed is a durable turn: when the log is absent or empty, exactly one\n        line â\x80\x94 the seed record â\x80\x94 is written before the first request is built.\n        ``max_length`` is a budget of generated responses, so the loop runs\n        ``while ledger.responses < self.max_length`` and the agent named by\n        ``ledger.next_speaker`` takes each turn.\n\n        Args:\n            working_dir (str): Directory where conversation results will be stored.\n\n        Returns:\n            Dataset: A HuggingFace Dataset containing the conversation history.\n        """\n        request_file = os.path.join(working_dir, RESPONSES_FILENAME)\n        os.makedirs(working_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n        # The seed is a durable turn: write it before the first request is built\n        # when the log is absent or empty.\n        log_exists = os.path.exists(request_file)\n        existing_records = read_log(working_dir) if log_exists else []\n        if not existing_records:\n            seed_record = build_seed_record(\n                seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\n                seed_message=self.seed_message,\n                model_name=self.seeder.model_name,\n                now=self.now_fn(),\n            )\n            async with aiofiles.open(request_file, "a") as f:\n                await self.append_response(self.seeder.name, f, seed_record)\n\n        # Derive resume state from the log instead of a step parity.\n        ledger = self.load_cache(working_dir)\n\n        # Adopt the ledger into the status tracker exactly once, before the\n        # completed short-circuit.\n        self.status_tracker.adopt_ledger(cached_responses=ledger.responses)\n\n        if ledger.completed:\n            self.status_tracker.stop_tracker()\n            return Dataset.from_file(self.create_dataset_file(working_dir))\n\n        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:\n            async with aiofiles.open(request_file, "a") as f:\n                while ledger.responses < self.max_length:\n                    speaker_name = ledger.next_speaker\n                    agent = self._agent_for(speaker_name)\n                    try:\n                        request = self._transform_conversation_history(agent)\n                        request = APIRequest(\n                            task_id=ledger.responses,\n                            generic_request=request,\n                            api_specific_request=agent._request_processor.create_api_specific_request_online(request),\n                            attempts_left=1,\n                            prompt_formatter=agent.prompt_formatter,\n                        )\n                        response = await agent._request_processor.call_single_request(request, session, status_tracker=None)\n                        await self.append_response(speaker_name, f, response)\n                        # Re-derive the ledger from the now-extended log.\n                        ledger = self.load_cache(working_dir)\n                        self.conversation_history = ledger.messages()\n                        self.status_tracker.update_turn(\n                            self._agent_turn_for(speaker_name),\n                            token_usage=response.token_usage,\n                            cost=response.response_cost,\n                        )\n                        if ledger.completed:\n                            break\n                    except Exception:\n                        # A failed turn appends nothing to the log and is recorded\n                        # against the agent named by next_speaker, not by step parity.\n                        self.status_tracker.update_turn(\n                            self._agent_turn_for(speaker_name), response_success=False\n                        )\n                        raise\n\n        self.status_tracker.stop_tracker()\n        return Dataset.from_file(self.create_dataset_file(working_dir))\n\n    async def append_response(self, name: str, f, response: GenericResponse) -> None:\n        """Append a response to the conversation history file.\n\n        Args:\n            name (str): The name of the agent generating the response.\n            f: The file object to write to.\n            response (GenericResponse): The response to append.\n        """\n        response = response.model_dump()\n        response["name"] = name\n        await f.write(json.dumps(response, default=str) + "\\n")\n\n    def _transform_conversation_history(self, target_agent: "Agent"):\n        """Transform the conversation history into a format suitable for the target agent.\n\n        Every ledger message is mapped to ``{"role": "assistant"}`` when its\n        author equals ``target.name`` and to ``{"role": "user"}`` otherwise,\n        uniformly and with no special case for a one-message history. The last\n        mapped message is replaced by the body produced by the target\'s\n        :meth:`PromptFormatter.create_generic_request`, and a single system\n        message (if any) is inserted at index 0. When the formatter produced\n        more than one system message, the first is used and the others stay in\n        place in the body.\n\n        Args:\n            target_agent (Agent): The agent for whom the conversation history is being transformed.\n\n        Returns:\n            GenericRequest: A request object containing the transformed conversation history.\n        """\n        transformed_conversation_history = []\n        for message in self.conversation_history:\n            if message["role"] == target_agent.name:\n                transformed_conversation_history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": message["content"]})\n            else:\n                transformed_conversation_history.append({"role": "user", "content": message["content"]})\n\n        request = target_agent.prompt_formatter.create_generic_request(\n            {"prompt": transformed_conversation_history[-1]["content"]}, 0\n        )\n        system_messages = [msg for msg in request.messages if msg["role"] == "system"]\n        body = [msg for msg in request.messages if msg["role"] != "system"]\n\n        # Replace the last mapped message with the formatter\'s body.\n        del transformed_conversation_history[-1]\n        transformed_conversation_history += body\n        request.messages = transformed_conversation_history\n\n        # Insert a single system message at index 0 only if there is one.\n        if system_messages:\n            request.messages.insert(0, system_messages[0])\n        return request\n\n    def create_dataset_file(self, working_dir: str) -> str:\n        """Create a dataset file from the conversation history.\n\n        Writes one row per log line with exactly four fields: ``role`` (the\n        record\'s name), ``content`` (``str(response_message)``), ``turn`` (the\n        0-based line index) and ``source`` (``"seed"`` for index 0, otherwise\n        ``"response"``). ``source`` is decided by position, not by\n        ``finish_reason``. Row order is log order.\n\n        Args:\n            working_dir (str): Directory where the dataset file will be created.\n\n        Returns:\n            str: Path to the created dataset file.\n        """\n        response_file = os.path.join(working_dir, RESPONSES_FILENAME)\n        dataset_file = os.path.join(working_dir, "dataset.arrow")\n\n        with ArrowWriter(path=dataset_file) as writer:\n            for index, record in enumerate(read_log(working_dir)):\n                content = record.response_message\n                if not isinstance(content, str):\n                    content = str(content)\n                source = SEED_FINISH_REASON if index == 0 else "response"\n                row = {\n                    "role": record.name,\n                    "content": content,\n                    "turn": index,\n                    "source": source,\n                }\n                writer.write(row)\n\n            # Finalize the writer\n            writer.finalize()\n\n        return dataset_file'}

1 seed + 6 responses\n\nasyncio.run(run())\nprint("RESUME OK")\nEOF\nHOME=/tmp/fakehome PYTHONPATH=src python3 /tmp/zzz_resume.py 2>&1 | grep -E "first run|resume|p2 ledger|^[0-9]+ |RESUME" | head -40'}

So `adopt_ledger` is NOT being called, or it's called with 0. Let me check — in `run`, after writing seed, `ledger.responses` should be 0 (only seed). Then `adopt_ledger(cached_responses=0)` sets `num_cached=0`, `current_turn=0`. Then the loop generates 4 responses. So at the end, `num_cached=0` (no inheritance, fresh run). 

That's actually correct behavior for a fresh run! `num_cached` describes what was *inherited*. On a fresh run, nothing is inherited (the seed isn't a "response"). The seed is a durable turn: write it before the first request is built\\n        # when the log is absent or empty.\\n        log_exists = os.path.exists(request_file)\\n        existing_records = read_log(working_dir) if log_exists else []\\n        if not existing_records:\\n            seed_record = build_seed_record(\\n                seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\\n                seed_message=self.seed_message,\\n                model_name=self.seeder.model_name,\\n                now=self.now_fn(),\\n            )\\n            async with aiofiles.open(request_file, \\"a\\") as f:\\n                await self.append_response(self.seeder.name, f, seed_record)\\n            existing_records = [seed_record]\\n        # Keep the in-memory record list in sync with the log on disk.\\n        self._records = list(existing_records)\\n        # Derive resume state from the log instead of a step parity.\\n        ledger = build_ledger(\\n            self._records,\\n            seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\\n            partner_name=self.partner.name,\\n            max_responses=self.max_length,\\n            is_completed=self._is_completed,\\n        )\\n        self.ledger = ledger\\n        self.conversation_history = ledger.messages()\\n\\n        # Adopt the ledger into the status tracker exactly once, before the\\n        # completed short-circuit.\\n        self.status_tracker.adopt_ledger(cached_responses=ledger.responses)\\n\\n        if ledger.completed:\\n            self.status_tracker.stop_tracker()\\n            return Dataset.from_file(self.create_dataset_file(working_dir))\\n",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

There's one subtle issue with the empty log + budget logic. Per the spec: "An empty log gives `(completion_reason, responses, turns, last_author, next_speaker) == ("open", 0, 0, None, None)` with `completed is False`."

But my code: for an empty log with `max_responses=0`, `responses=0 >= 0` → budget → completed=True, next_speaker=None. This contradicts the "empty log gives open" requirement when max_responses > 0.

Wait, let me re-read the spec: "An empty log gives `("open", 0, 0, None, None)`". This must be for a *normal* budget (>0). With max_responses=0 and empty log, budget applies. But the spec states this unconditionally for an empty log.

Actually, the agent_signal check requires `entries` to be non-empty (the last entry's author). For an empty log, there's no last entry, so agent_signal is impossible. The budget check: "otherwise `"budget"` when `responses >= max_responses`". For an empty log, responses=0. If max_responses=0, then 0>=0 → budget. If max_responses>0, open.

The spec's "empty log gives open" is the common case (max_responses > 0). My code handles that correctly. For max_responses=0, budget applies, which is consistent with the budget rule. This is a reasonable interpretation.

Let me verify my empty-log test (max_responses=4) gives open — already tested and confirmed. Good.

Now let me double-check one final concern: the `is_completed` callable signature is `Callable[[str, t.Any], bool]` — `(author, content)`. In my processor `_is_completed(author, content)` calls `agent.is_completed(content)`. The `Agent.is_completed(self, response)` only takes content. That's consistent — the ledger passes `(author, content)` and the processor's `_is_completed` adapter looks up the agent by author and calls `agent.is_completed(content)`.

## Summary

I implemented the durable, resumable multi-turn agent conversation record. Here are all the changes:

### New module: `src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/turn_ledger.py`
- Module docstring `"""The durable record of a multi-turn agent conversation."""`
- Imports only `datetime`, `os`, `typing`, `dataclasses`, pydantic `ValidationError`, and existing `AgentResponse`/`GenericRequest` â no `time`/`random`/`uuid`/`os.urandom`.
- Constants: `RESPONSES_FILENAME`, `SEED_FINISH_REASON`, `COMPLETION_SENTINEL`, `COMPLETION_REASONS`, `LEDGER_STATUSES`.
- Exceptions: `TurnLedgerError(RuntimeError)` and `TurnLedgerCorruptError` with `.path`, `.line_number` (1-based), `.reason`.
- Two **frozen dataclasses** `TurnEntry` and `TurnLedger` (entries is a `tuple`), plus `TurnLedger.messages()`.
- Functions `build_seed_record`, `read_log` (skips blank lines, raises `TurnLedgerCorruptError` for unparseable lines), `build_ledger` (derives resume state from the log: `responses==len(entries)-1`, `next_speaker` = complement of last author, `interleave_faults`, `completion_reason` checked on the last entry only with agent_signal winning), and `load_ledger` (read-only).

### `src/bespokelabs/curator/status_tracker/agent_status_tracker.py`
- New fields `num_cached`, `time_fn`; `start_time`/`last_update_time` now come from `time_fn`.
- `update_turn` now increments only `num_errors` (leaves `current_turn`/`num_responses` alone), still sets `current_agent` and accumulates tokens/cost.
- New `adopt_ledger(cached_responses=...)` resets per-process counters, sets `current_turn`/`num_cached`, leaves `max_turns` alone, doesn't call `update_display`.
- `stop_tracker` pops `"time_fn"` from telemetry metadata alongside `"pbar"`.

### `src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/agent.py`
- `Agent.is_completed` now has a real default implementation in terms of `COMPLETION_SENTINEL` (coerces non-strings).
- `MultiTurnAgents.__init__` accepts and forwards `now_fn`.

### `src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/agent_response.py`
- `update_tracker_stats` reports `cached=tracker.num_cached`.

### `src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/processor.py`
- `__init__` keeps `self.now_fn` and `self.ledger`.
- `load_cache` returns a `TurnLedger`, doesn't touch the status tracker.
- The seed is a durable turn written via `append_response()` before the first request; `max_length` is a budget of generated responses (`while ledger.responses < self.max_length`); `APIRequest(task_id=ledger.responses)`; each iteration uses `ledger.next_speaker`; `_agent_for` maps names; `run` short-circuits on `ledger.completed`; `adopt_ledger` called once before the short-circuit; failed turns append nothing and are recorded against `next_speaker`.
- `_transform_conversation_history` maps uniformly (no one-message special case), inserts only a single system message, keeps extra system messages in the body.
- `create_dataset_file` writes 4 fields (`role`, `content`, `turn`, `source`) per log line, `source` decided by position.
